above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_118d0db644faf6f3c909","text":"3. **A restorative cry is darkly reversed.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ع ع/B004` is the cry said to a stumbler, “rise” or “recover,” while fixed `ي ت م/B004` includes kindness delayed from the orphan. **Structural cue:** the same root that can accompany raising a fallen person here governs the orphan as the object of a harsh transitive act; later non-urging and withholding prolong the delay. **Assigned role:** the recovery branch marks the help that this encounter could have supplied. **Abductive move:** the packet does not call the orphan a literal stumbler; I infer social stumbling from lost protection and postponed care. **Reading change:** `يدع اليتيم` reads as the reversal of restoration: an occasion to raise the exposed person is converted into renewed rejection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B003","root_001692/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1bfb1867821ac4526a5e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:2:4\",\"root_001692\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yetimle ilgili gecikme ve eksik kalma boyutu, sonraki namaz umursamazlığıyla aynı dikkat bozukluğunu açığa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1d64540f9595ac654333","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:2:3\",\"root_000477\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bir kabı doldurmaya yarayabilecek hareketin tersine, burada güç kaynakları ihtiyacın çevresinde toplamak yerine ihtiyaç sahibini uzaklaştırmak için kullanılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B002","root_001692/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30056796e1134d10277e","text":"107:3 first exposed a reversal I could not see from the thrust alone: the force of `د ع ع/B002` that can pack a vessel toward fullness is followed by refusal to mobilize food. 107:7 then completed the correction by making delayed care (`ي ت م/B004`) an active blockade, showing that the push is one moment in a longer system of sustained isolation rather than a self-contained outburst.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B001","root_001692/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30b793bf089f5358c28d","text":"1. **The thrust actively reproduces orphanhood.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ع ع/B001` is rough thrusting, harsh driving, and pushing someone away from a due right; fixed `ي ت م/B001` is offspring cut off from a protecting parent. **Structural cue:** `فذلك` makes this action the identifying consequence of 107:1, while 107:3 and 107:7 continue the pattern of blocked provision. **Assigned role:** the thrust is not incidental violence; it extends the orphan's prior severance into a present social exclusion. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies bereavement, force, and later withholding; I infer that driving the child away causes continued loss of substitute protection. **Reading change:** the subject does not merely encounter an orphan but helps manufacture the orphan's ongoing social isolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3a71410bed026f9a6ed6","text":"4. **`اليتيم` names a condition that society can maintain.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م/B002` is isolated singleness, and fixed `ي ت م/B004` is slowness or delay in care. **Structural cue:** after the initial loss encoded by the noun, 107:3 adds failure to exhort, 107:5 adds heedlessness, and 107:7 adds active withholding. **Assigned role:** singleness describes the social position; delay describes the temporal process that keeps it in place. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies repeated omissions by the surrounding group, but the arrow from those omissions to sustained isolation is my inference. **Reading change:** “orphan” is not only a static biographical status; the ayah exposes an orphaning process continually renewed by delayed communal care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_003249/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_58a4b2fd8299f91a03eb","text":"The later `س ه و B001` surprised me by activating `ي ت م B003/B004`: orphan-care is delayed or missed before prayer is missed. The final `م ن ع B002` also clarified the shove as barrier-making, not only momentary violence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B002","root_000934/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_84353f340733c236960b","text":"2. **Force used for expulsion is the inverse of force used for filling.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د ع ع/B002` is agitating a measure, vessel, or sack until it fills; 107:3 supplies `ط ع م/B002`, feeding another, and 107:7 supplies `م ع ن/B005`, small household benefit, gift, or alms made available. **Structural cue:** rough movement of the child is followed by refusal to mobilize food and finally by withholding useful goods. **Assigned role:** the vessel branch supplies a material logistics model in which motion should make provision fit and become full. **Abductive move:** the packet does not equate the child with a vessel; I infer a directional reversal: the agent applies force to eject a dependent where social force could instead organize filling and supply. **Reading change:** `يدع` becomes an anti-provisioning action, displacing the recipient rather than arranging resources around the need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B001","root_001692/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_950406c9a375252f8105","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:2:3\",\"root_000477\",[\"B001\"]],[\"107:2:4\",\"root_001692\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İtme, koruyucusunu yitirmiş çocuğun önceki kopuşunu yeniden üretir ve onu hak ettiği desteğin önünde kalıcı bir engelle karşı karşıya bırakır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B002","root_000934/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a4db798044323f131067","text":"3. **Resource-packing without mercy.** Evidence: `د ع ع B002` agitating a vessel until it fills; activated by `ط ع م B004` provision/livelihood and `م ع ن B005` small available benefit. Structure: the same root that can move a container toward fullness appears in an ayah about pushing away need. Mechanism: goods can be packed and retained while the dependent is driven off. Abductive move: I supply the economic inversion: movement that should help a vessel receive becomes social violence that keeps resources inside. Reading change: يدع becomes a pressure of hoarding as much as a shove.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c7ff9ff44f9f2b3b65a1","text":"1. **The orphan is physically and legally thrust away.** Evidence: `د ع ع B001` rough thrusting or forceful driving, `ي ت م B001` offspring cut from protecting parent; activated by `م ن ع B002` barrier between a person and what he seeks in 107:7. Structure: pushing comes early; explicit withholding comes last. Mechanism: the push is not only bodily harshness but the creation of a barrier between the orphan and due support. Abductive move: I supply the connection from physical force to social/legal exclusion; the packet supplies thrust, orphan-cutoff, and barrier branches. Reading change: يدع اليتيم reads as active displacement from protection, not merely rudeness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B002","root_001692/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d0450f26b1c647e09e79","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:2:4\",\"root_001692\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yetimlik burada yalnız biyografik bir durum değildir; geciken bakım ve yinelenen dışlama, çocuğun toplumsal yalnızlığını sürdüren bir yetimleştirme sürecidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B003","root_003249/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_db593a9b2b7350b7f0cb","text":"4. **Heedless shortfall around the orphan.** Evidence: `ي ت م B003` heedlessness/falling short and `ي ت م B004` delayed kindness; activated by `س ه و B001` heedlessness in 107:5. Structure: later “heedless of prayer” echoes a dormant orphan-root branch. Mechanism: ritual inattention and social delay are the same posture of missed attention. Abductive move: I infer a parallel between failure to attend to prayer and failure to attend to the orphan; the packet supplies both heedlessness branches. Reading change: the orphan ayah becomes an early sign of the later prayer-defect.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e493ae79436f7c8a867f","text":"2. **Orphaning as intensified isolation.** Evidence: `ي ت م B002` isolated singleness/peerless separation, with `ي ت م B004` delay in care; activated by `س ك ن B003` household inhabitants and `م ع ن B006` dwelling/stopping-place. Structure: household/dwelling roots later define what the orphan lacks. Mechanism: the denier converts a child already cut off into a socially un-housed solitary figure. Abductive move: I infer that the later domestic lexicon casts the orphan as excluded from household circulation. Reading change: the ayah becomes about isolating the vulnerable from the house, not only pushing one child.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B004","root_001692/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ff96975ad369383d3787","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:2:3\",\"root_000477\",[\"B004\"]],[\"107:2:4\",\"root_001692\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Düşeni ayağa kaldıran bir çağrının tersine çevrilmesi gibi, korunmasız çocuğu toparlama fırsatı yeni bir geri çevirmeye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
